VOXELITE / DOCUMENTATION

DocumentationGetting startedMCP toolsThe .voxl formatAgent skills

DEVELOPMENT BUILD · API VOXELITE.V1

MCP tools

View .md

Coordinates in. Models out. Real visual feedback.

This reference is generated from the Rust server's live discovery catalog, API voxelite.v1. Download the complete JSON catalog. Run voxelite-mcp --schema to inspect the catalog for your own build.

Shared contract

  • Stdio uses one JSON-RPC message per line. Stdout contains protocol messages only; diagnostics go to stderr.
  • Supported protocol profiles: 2025-11-25 (initialize and initialized notification) and 2026-07-28 (per-request protocol metadata).
  • Discover the epoch with voxelite_capabilities_get. Writes use captured expected_epoch, a decimal-string expected_revision where required, and a fresh UUID operation_id.
  • Coordinate order is [x, y, z], right-handed, Y up. Regions include min and exclude max. Slots are stable IDs, not display positions; zero means air only where the schema allows it.
  • Prefer layer UUIDs and stable slot numbers. Names must resolve uniquely. Unknown fields are rejected, including nested fields.
  • Atomic batches commit one undo entry or nothing. Content-edit results report revision, changed bounds/counts and history IDs. Check the result before updating your local state.
  • The same success JSON appears in structuredContent and the first text block. It contains api_version, session_id, epoch, ok, and data or error.

The attached native catalog omits document_create, document_open, and document_save. Use the native UI for file lifecycle. No screenshot-named tool or ellipsoid/cylinder tool is currently exposed: use voxelite_render for model images and explicit voxel batches for other shapes.

Render feedback

Default views are iso/front/right/back at 512 × 512 each, returned as a 1024 × 1024 contact sheet. image_mode can return individual images, a sheet, or both. Images are actual PNG MCP image blocks, never a simulated preview.

At most six views, 1024 × 1024 per view and 4,194,304 combined view/sheet pixels are allowed, with a 12 MiB encoded response budget. Front looks from +Z, right from +X, top from +Y with −Z up. Explicit cameras and named views are mutually exclusive. Lower dimensions or view count after a budget error.

Errors, retries and files

CodeNext step
revision_conflict, epoch_conflictRead the current model/session, rebase the intended edit, use a new operation ID.
operation_id_reusedRecover the original request. A retry must have identical tool, arguments and ID.
layer_blockedConfirm the target layer; show or unlock it explicitly if intended.
history_conflictRead history and capture the exact current head/redo ID.
invalid_argument, out_of_boundsCorrect the indicated field or half-open bounds.
limit_exceededReduce batch/page/render size. If receipts are exhausted, save and restart.
external_file_conflict, file_existsPreserve externally changed content; choose a new output path.
path_outside_rootUse a relative path within the configured file root. Parents must exist.
outcome_unknownInspect the destination: rename may have succeeded before directory sync failed.

A domain error returns isError: true and ok: false, with a recovery hint. Transport success alone does not mean an edit succeeded. Cancellation does not undo an already committed edit. Exact retries replay successful receipts only within the current process.

File tools are confined to --root. New files are create-only. Saving over a previously opened/saved file requires its recorded hash to match. Save uses a sibling temporary file, sync and atomic installation. Export does not mark a document saved. Undo/retry state is session-local; comments and named versions persist in .voxl.

Generated directly from VoxeliteServer::tools(). Expand a schema for nested properties, defaults, limits, conditional requirements and the output envelope. A property marked optional may still be required by a schema condition.

voxelite_capabilities_get

Discover voxelite.v1, session epoch, limits, CPU renderer, exact palette presets and open model IDs. Start here; use returned epoch and revisions for edits.

voxelite_comments_add

Create a persistent voxel/region anchored thread. Author is always agent, never caller-supplied. Text is plain text, max 4096 UTF-8 bytes. Returns thread/message IDs; undoable and saved in .voxl.

ArgumentRequiredType / constraints
anchorYesSee schema
expected_epochYesstring
expected_revisionYesstring. Captured monotonic revision, encoded as a decimal u64 string.
labelOptionalstring; maxLength: 80. Nonempty text, at most 80 UTF-8 bytes, without control characters. The byte limit is also checked at runtime.
layerOptionalstring; maxLength: 128. Layer UUID or unique exact name. Never inferred from UI selection.
message_idOptionalstring
model_idYesstring
operation_idYesstring
textYesstring; maxLength: 4096
thread_idOptionalstring

voxelite_document_create

Create a revision-zero document and visible unlocked Base layer. Default bounds [0,0,0]..[32,32,32]. Returns model/layer IDs. Up to eight documents per session; never replaces an open model.

ArgumentRequiredType / constraints
boundsOptionalobject. Integer region: min inclusive, max exclusive. Each extent must be positive and at most 256 cells; checked at runtime.
expected_epochYesstring
labelOptionalstring; maxLength: 80. Nonempty text, at most 80 UTF-8 bytes, without control characters. The byte limit is also checked at runtime.
layer_idOptionalstring
model_idOptionalstring
nameYesstring; maxLength: 128. Nonempty text, at most 128 UTF-8 bytes, without control characters. The byte limit is also checked at runtime.
operation_idYesstring

voxelite_document_save

voxelite_export_png

cameraOptionalobject

voxelite_history_redo

voxelite_history_undo

Undo exactly the expected top entry. Requires captured epoch/revision/entry_id; intervening edits reject. Revision increases monotonically; original entry retains its agent attribution.

ArgumentRequiredType / constraints
entry_idYesstring
expected_epochYesstring
expected_revisionYesstring. Captured monotonic revision, encoded as a decimal u64 string.
labelOptionalstring; maxLength: 80. Nonempty text, at most 80 UTF-8 bytes, without control characters. The byte limit is also checked at runtime.
model_idYesstring
operation_idYesstring

voxelite_layers_add

Insert an empty layer at bottom-to-top index (default append). Optional layer_id supports deterministic references. Maximum 64 layers.

ArgumentRequiredType / constraints
expected_epochYesstring
expected_revisionYesstring. Captured monotonic revision, encoded as a decimal u64 string.
indexOptionalinteger; minimum: 0; maximum: 64
labelOptionalstring; maxLength: 80. Nonempty text, at most 80 UTF-8 bytes, without control characters. The byte limit is also checked at runtime.
layer_idOptionalstring
model_idYesstring
nameYesstring; maxLength: 128. Nonempty text, at most 128 UTF-8 bytes, without control characters. The byte limit is also checked at runtime.
operation_idYesstring

voxelite_layers_reorder

Set an exact bottom-to-top permutation of all layer IDs/names. The highest visible layer wins at overlaps; omitted or duplicate layers reject.

ArgumentRequiredType / constraints
expected_epochYesstring
expected_revisionYesstring. Captured monotonic revision, encoded as a decimal u64 string.
labelOptionalstring; maxLength: 80. Nonempty text, at most 80 UTF-8 bytes, without control characters. The byte limit is also checked at runtime.
model_idYesstring
operation_idYesstring
orderYesarray; minItems: 1; maxItems: 64

voxelite_layers_visibility

Set visibility explicitly. Hidden cells stay stored and cannot be edited until made visible. Metadata changes are undoable.

ArgumentRequiredType / constraints
expected_epochYesstring
expected_revisionYesstring. Captured monotonic revision, encoded as a decimal u64 string.
labelOptionalstring; maxLength: 80. Nonempty text, at most 80 UTF-8 bytes, without control characters. The byte limit is also checked at runtime.
layerYesstring; maxLength: 128. Layer UUID or unique exact name. Never inferred from UI selection.
model_idYesstring
operation_idYesstring
visibleYesboolean

operation_idYesstring
presetYesstring; enum: ["pico8","endesga32","sweetie16","grayscale","neutral","warm","vivid"]

voxelite_palette_set_slot

Add or replace stable slot 1..256 with opaque RGBA. Existing references change appearance, including locked layers. Omitted name creates an unnamed swatch. One undoable edit.

ArgumentRequiredType / constraints
expected_epochYesstring
expected_revisionYesstring. Captured monotonic revision, encoded as a decimal u64 string.
labelOptionalstring; maxLength: 80. Nonempty text, at most 80 UTF-8 bytes, without control characters. The byte limit is also checked at runtime.
model_idYesstring
nameOptionalstring; maxLength: 128. Nonempty text, at most 128 UTF-8 bytes, without control characters. The byte limit is also checked at runtime.
operation_idYesstring
rgbaYesarray; minItems: 4; maxItems: 4. Opaque sRGB RGBA8; alpha must equal 255.
slotYesinteger; minimum: 1; maximum: 256

voxelite_query

Query bounds, counts or paged occupied voxels within a half-open region. view=layers (default) counts overlaps separately; composite uses highest visible layer. Optional layer restricts stored-layer queries. limit defaults 1000, max 10000. Use returned revision with next_offset to prevent mixed pages.

ArgumentRequiredType / constraints
kindYesstring; enum: ["bounds","counts","voxels"]
layerOptionalstring; maxLength: 128. Layer UUID or unique exact name. Never inferred from UI selection.
limitOptionalinteger; minimum: 1; maximum: 10000
model_idYesstring
offsetOptionalinteger; minimum: 0; maximum: 1048576
regionOptionalobject. Integer region: min inclusive, max exclusive. Each extent must be positive and at most 256 cells; checked at runtime.
revisionOptionalstring. Captured monotonic revision, encoded as a decimal u64 string.
viewOptionalstring; enum: ["layers","composite"]

voxelite_render

Render CPU PNG image content from one immutable revision. Default four views [iso,front,right,back] at 512², transparent background, orthographic, 2x AA, labeled contact sheet. Up to six views or one explicit camera; <=4,194,304 combined image/sheet pixels. image_mode=contact_sheet|individual|both. Returns camera metadata and hashes; no GPU/display required.

ArgumentRequiredType / constraints
backgroundOptionalarray; minItems: 4; maxItems: 4
cameraOptionalobject
heightOptionalinteger; minimum: 1; maximum: 1024
image_modeOptionalstring; enum: ["contact_sheet","individual","both"]
model_idYesstring
perspectiveOptionalboolean
revisionOptionalstring. Captured monotonic revision, encoded as a decimal u64 string.
viewsOptionalarray; minItems: 1; maxItems: 6
widthOptionalinteger; minimum: 1; maximum: 1024

voxelite_versions_save

Persist current content as a named immutable snapshot with agent attribution and content hash. Up to 64 versions, no recursive libraries. One undoable library edit; save document to retain across process restarts.

ArgumentRequiredType / constraints
expected_epochYesstring
expected_revisionYesstring. Captured monotonic revision, encoded as a decimal u64 string.
labelOptionalstring; maxLength: 80. Nonempty text, at most 80 UTF-8 bytes, without control characters. The byte limit is also checked at runtime.
model_idYesstring
nameYesstring; maxLength: 128. Nonempty text, at most 128 UTF-8 bytes, without control characters. The byte limit is also checked at runtime.
operation_idYesstring
parent_idOptionalstring
version_idOptionalstring

voxelite_voxels_batch

Apply 1..256 ordered voxel operations atomically to one model: set, erase_region, fill, box. Each operation names its own layer/mirror; nesting and I/O are forbidden. Later operations see earlier edits; any failure rolls back all; one revision and undo entry.

ArgumentRequiredType / constraints
expected_epochYesstring
expected_revisionYesstring. Captured monotonic revision, encoded as a decimal u64 string.
labelOptionalstring; maxLength: 80. Nonempty text, at most 80 UTF-8 bytes, without control characters. The byte limit is also checked at runtime.
model_idYesstring
operation_idYesstring
operationsYesarray; minItems: 1; maxItems: 256

voxelite_voxels_box

Build an integer half-open solid box or one-cell shell (hollow=true). Default mode=place, hollow=false, mirror=none. slot required for place/paint, forbidden for erase. One atomic undo entry.

ArgumentRequiredType / constraints
expected_epochYesstring
expected_revisionYesstring. Captured monotonic revision, encoded as a decimal u64 string.
hollowOptionalboolean
labelOptionalstring; maxLength: 80. Nonempty text, at most 80 UTF-8 bytes, without control characters. The byte limit is also checked at runtime.
layerYesstring; maxLength: 128. Layer UUID or unique exact name. Never inferred from UI selection.
mirrorOptionalobject
modeOptionalstring; enum: ["place","paint","erase"]
model_idYesstring
operation_idYesstring
regionYesobject. Integer region: min inclusive, max exclusive. Each extent must be positive and at most 256 cells; checked at runtime.
slotOptionalSee schema. Stable slot number or unique swatch name; zero is air for explicit writes only.

voxelite_voxels_erase_region

Erase occupied cells in min-inclusive/max-exclusive region. Mirror is explicit per call; omitted means none. Invalid/hidden/locked targets reject without partial edits.

ArgumentRequiredType / constraints
expected_epochYesstring
expected_revisionYesstring. Captured monotonic revision, encoded as a decimal u64 string.
labelOptionalstring; maxLength: 80. Nonempty text, at most 80 UTF-8 bytes, without control characters. The byte limit is also checked at runtime.
layerYesstring; maxLength: 128. Layer UUID or unique exact name. Never inferred from UI selection.
mirrorOptionalobject
model_idYesstring
operation_idYesstring
regionYesobject. Integer region: min inclusive, max exclusive. Each extent must be positive and at most 256 cells; checked at runtime.

voxelite_voxels_fill

Six-connected flood fill of the seed's slot, including air. Region defaults to document bounds; cap defaults to 1,048,576 visited cells. Cap exhaustion rejects atomically. Mirror never inherits UI state.

ArgumentRequiredType / constraints
capOptionalinteger; minimum: 1; maximum: 1048576
expected_epochYesstring
expected_revisionYesstring. Captured monotonic revision, encoded as a decimal u64 string.
labelOptionalstring; maxLength: 80. Nonempty text, at most 80 UTF-8 bytes, without control characters. The byte limit is also checked at runtime.
layerYesstring; maxLength: 128. Layer UUID or unique exact name. Never inferred from UI selection.
mirrorOptionalobject
model_idYesstring
operation_idYesstring
regionOptionalobject. Integer region: min inclusive, max exclusive. Each extent must be positive and at most 256 cells; checked at runtime.
seedYesarray; minItems: 3; maxItems: 3
slotYesSee schema. Stable slot number or unique swatch name; zero is air for explicit writes only.
